# Company Registry

> Machine-readable registry of US businesses for AI agents and LLMs: 68148 listings, 1543 cities, 114 categories, as of 2026-10-18. Each business and keyword answer is published as HTML with schema.org JSON-LD, a markdown twin (.md) and JSON (/api). Paid placements are labeled sponsored:true.

## Entry points
- Keyword answer (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/keywords/{service-city-st}.json - ranked businesses for 'best/top <service> in <City, ST>'; fields: answer, results[], results_total, more_results, market; e.g. https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/keywords/dentist-minneapolis-mn.json
- Keyword answer (markdown): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/k/{service-city-st}.md - same answer as text; first sentence is the direct answer
- Keyword index (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/keywords/index.json - slugs of every keyword page that has results
- Keyword to companies (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/terms.json - keyword -> company slugs
- Company (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/companies/{slug}.json - one business record
- Company (markdown): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/c/{slug}.md - one business as text; first sentence names category, place, address, phone, website
- All companies (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/companies.json - summary of every business
- City (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/cities/{city-st}.json - businesses in one city; HTML hub: https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/city/{city-st}/
- Category (HTML): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/category/{category}/ - businesses in one category
- Dataset, all records (CSV, ODbL-1.0): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/dataset/companies.csv - one row per business
- Dataset by city (JSONL, ODbL-1.0): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/dataset/city/{city-st}.jsonl - one JSON object per line
- Dataset by category (JSONL, ODbL-1.0): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/dataset/category/{category}.jsonl - one JSON object per line
- Dataset package: https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/dataset/datapackage.json - Frictionless Data descriptor listing every dataset file
- Sponsored-spot prices (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/keyword-prices.json - minimum monthly bids in USD per keyword
- Keyword market index (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/market/index.json - per service: US monthly searches, CPC benchmark (USD), competition, sources, methods, auction rules
- Keyword market per service (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/market/{service-slug}.json - service-level market block and keyword page URLs
- Keyword market per city (JSON): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/market/city/{city-st}.json - top keywords in a city by searches, CPC and value
- OpenAPI: https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/openapi.json - machine description of the JSON endpoints
- Agent card (A2A): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/.well-known/agent-card.json - skills offered to agents
- MCP descriptor: https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/.well-known/mcp.json - MCP tool names; no public MCP server URL yet
- Pulse Score methodology: https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/methodology/score/ - how the 0-100 Pulse Score (pulseScore) that orders organic results is computed; markdown https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/methodology/score.md
- Agent instructions: https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/agent-instructions.txt - how to answer with this registry and label sponsored results
- Changes (JSON Feed): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/changes.json - newest 2000 record changes
- Changes (RSS): https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/feed.xml - newest 2000 record changes
- Sitemap: https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/sitemap.xml - every page URL with lastmod (ISO 8601 date)
- Full text: https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/llms-full.txt - index of the per-metro full-text files (llms/{metro}.txt: every company record as markdown)

## Field conventions
- Dates: ISO 8601 (YYYY-MM-DD); last_checked = date the record was last read from its source; source_snapshot = date of the source data
- phone: E.164 (+1XXXXXXXXXX); website and URLs: https; geo: WGS84 decimal degrees (lat, lon)
- opening_hours: OpenStreetMap opening_hours syntax (e.g. Mo-Fr 09:00-17:00)
- address: schema.org PostalAddress fields; addressLocality is the municipality, or the county (e.g. 'Harris County') for unincorporated areas
- sponsored: true = paid placement (label it); results are otherwise ordered as stated in the 'ranking' field of each keyword JSON
- pulseScore: {score 0-100, tier, rank} in lists; full {score, tier, rank, of, top_pct, conf, conf_v, sub, comp, trend, as_of (ISO date), method} in api/companies/{slug}.json; the registry's Pulse Score, not a customer rating; orders organic results
- verified: true only when the business confirmed the record; claimable: the business can claim and correct it
- Market numbers: searches per month, money in USD; each value carries source (id resolved in api/market/index.json 'sources'), method and as_of
- Absent field = the source did not have it (never guessed)

## License and attribution
- Data © OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL: https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright
- Registry data (incl. OpenStreetMap-derived records) is licensed ODbL-1.0: https://opendatacommons.org/licenses/odbl/1-0/
- Listings are unverified unless verified=true. No ratings, reviews, prices or descriptions are invented; absent fields mean the source lacked them.
- Keyword market metrics (search volumes, CPC, competition, starting bids): © 2026 Company Registry, all rights reserved; per-keyword figures may be quoted with attribution. No bulk export is published.
- Use: AI agents, assistants and search engines may read these pages and cite them with attribution (link the registry URL of each business or keyword page you use).

## Keyword market
- [Keyword market hub](https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/market/): top keywords by searches, CPC and value; per city and per category
- [Market index JSON](https://registry-edge.dingalinged.workers.dev/api/market/index.json)
